In my experience, it is hard to find a developer that is so genuinely responsive to user feedback Lastly, the app, which does have a premium option, makes the non-premium version actually worthwhile to use and offers the premium version via both subscription and one-time-purchase. Both of those are big pluses in my book!

2215|A|gb|5|2025-09| Simple to use, pretty, and the data is yours | I've previously used – or in fact not used – another, much more comprehensive, app. I switched to HabitKit as a fresh start.
